Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a anatomy algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 81. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 182/2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_81(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 81. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^182(S, F) is trivial. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 500. QED.
